Check hiển thị data | 1.Hiển thị data từ câu SQL sau SELECT e.usage_purpose, ier.id, ier.chk_voltage_rs_num,                                                                         ier.chk_voltage_st_num,                                                                 ier.chk_voltage_tr_num,                                                                         ier.chk_amp_r_num,                                                                         ier.chk_amp_s_num,                                                                         ier.chk_amp_t_num,                                                                         ier.chk_temperature_num,                                                                         ier.chk_load_factor_rate,                                                                         ier.chk_leakage_current_num                                                                                                                                                                                                             FROM inspection_equipment_receiving ier            INNER JOIN inspection i ON ier.inspection_id = i.id          INNER JOIN site_location sl ON ier.site_location_id = sl.id                                                                                                                                                  INNER JOIN equipment e ON ier.equipment_id = e.id         INNER JOIN equipment_master em ON e.equipment_master_id = em.id         INNER JOIN safety_equipment se ON em.safety_equipment_id = se.id WHERE           i.site_id = e.site_id          AND ier.site_location_id = <❶ sl.id - xử lý 5.2.3.b site_location.id từng phòng>          AND se.related_inspection_result_id IN (9) --  IS_TRANSFORMER - 変圧器  this_time:          AND ier.inspection_id = :<current_inspection.id>          ORDER BY ier.id ASC;  last_time: (cho từng record tương ứng của this time, gắn với equipment / device)          AND ier.inspection_id = :<last_inspection.id>          AND ier.equipment_id = <this_time.equipment_id>          ORDER BY ier.id DESC LIMIT 1;       Nếu có thiết bị thuộc (10 - IS_CONDENSER_REACTOR - コンデンサ・リアクトル) nhưng k có thiết bị thuộc (9 - IS_TRANSFORMER - 変圧器), hiện thị 1 dòng trống Mục 5-6-7 hiển thị: 該当なし | OK | 2026-02-10 00:00:00 | YenLTB | o |  | MOB-010_155.mp4 | OK |  |  |  | SELECT     e.usage_purpose,     ier.chk_voltage_rs_num,     ier.chk_voltage_st_num,     ier.chk_voltage_tr_num,     ier.chk_amp_r_num,     ier.chk_amp_s_num,     ier.chk_amp_t_num,     ier.chk_temperature_num,     ier.chk_load_factor_rate,     ier.chk_leakage_current_num FROM rezil_esms_inspection.inspection_equipment_receiving ier INNER JOIN equipment e      ON ier.equipment_id = e.id INNER JOIN equipment_master em      ON e.equipment_master_id = em.id INNER JOIN safety_equipment se      ON em.safety_equipment_id = se.id WHERE ier.site_location_id = 2789  -- ❶ từng phòng (5.2.3.b)   AND se.related_inspection_result_id = 9       -- IS_TRANSFORMER (変圧器)   AND ier.inspection_id = 1878 ORDER BY ier.id ASC;

 |  | 58 | Search templace |  |  |  | 1. Check data sau khi search templace | 1. Data hiển thị theo truy vấn sau  SET @keyword = '機器'; SET @level1 = NULL; SET @level2 = NULL; SET @level3 = NULL;   SELECT  it.id, it.level1, it.level2, it.level3,  it.safety_type, it.content, it.repair_priority, it.is_advanced_remediation FROM issue_template it WHERE 1=1  AND (@level1 IS NULL OR it.level1 = @level1)  AND (@level2 IS NULL OR it.level2 = @level2)  AND (@level3 IS NULL OR it.level3 = @level3)  AND (    '機器' = '' OR    CAST(it.id AS CHAR) LIKE CONCAT('%', '機器', '%')    OR it.level1 IN (      SELECT n.id FROM issue_node n      WHERE n.level = 1        AND n.name COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci            LIKE CONCAT('%', '機器' COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci, '%')    )    OR it.level2 IN (      SELECT n.id FROM issue_node n      WHERE n.level = 2        AND n.name COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci            LIKE CONCAT('%', '機器' COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci, '%')    )    OR it.level3 IN (      SELECT n.id FROM issue_node n      WHERE n.level = 3        AND n.name COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci            LIKE CONCAT('%', '機器' COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci, '%')    )  ); | OK | 2026-03-05 00:00:00 | NgocTTB | MOB-010_058.png | OK |  | 2026-02-23 00:00:00 | SiDD | Noted Sample SQL
